A global confidence threshold cannot separate these: it either admits unsupported value claims or discards well-evidenced ones. Conditioning the threshold on category resolves the tradeoff. In repeated held-out evaluation, a stricter bar on values alone reduces unsupported retentions from 6.2\% to 4.0\% (an ${\approx}36\%$ relative reduction, modest but consistent across folds) and, as corroborating evidence, preserves an estimated 13 percentage points more coverage (95\% CI 9.8--16.0) than a global threshold at comparable retention. Our results suggest that reliable retention depends on the type of assertion, not on confidence alone, and that a category-conditioned threshold can act as a simple, effective form of selective prediction at the write boundary.

</details>


### [237] [AMBER: Training Long-Horizon Web Agents through Append-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07118)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Zhaoyu Zhang,Mingyu Zhao,Shuang Xie,Han Li,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AMBER（仅追加记忆库）框架，让语言模型智能体联合学习推理、行动与自由形式记忆写入，以仅追加规则保证长程信息保留，并通过结果奖励端到端强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多步任务中交互历史迅速超出上下文预算，智能体需在长程上保持事实、记住执行错误与纠正反馈并跟踪进度。现有覆盖式记忆理论上能保留任何信息，但须学会在每次重写中携带每个事实，从稀疏结果奖励中难以学习；在 Web 智能体等交互场景中，训练后的覆盖式记忆会删除轨迹所需关键信息和环境纠正反馈。

Method: AMBER 采用仅追加记忆库：智能体联合学习推理、行动并写入自由形式记忆，借助仅追加规则从构造上保证信息保留。由此可直接以结果奖励进行端到端强化学习，无需大量人工整理的监督微调数据。

Result: 在 WebArena Lite 上，AMBER 相比覆盖式记忆基线平均成功率提升 4.09 个百分点；五次重复运行中完全解决任务的比例提升 4.8 个百分点；并达到与使用更昂贵人工监督训练的覆盖式基线相当的水平，同时维持实用的 token 预算。

Conclusion: 仅追加记忆以构造性保留替代学习式保留，在上下文效率、任务性能与可靠长程执行之间取得良好平衡，为长程语言模型智能体提供简单可扩展的记忆方案。

Abstract: Modern language-model agents increasingly interact with external environments over long-horizon, multi-step trajectories, where the accumulated interaction history can quickly exceed practical context budgets. To ensure reliability, agents must maintain factual information over long horizons, remember execution errors and corrective feedback, and track progress across actions. Several approaches have been proposed to achieve this without the need for maintaining the entire execution history in context, such as using the reasoning and action history, learning to maintain a fixed-size memory through an overwrite mechanism, and periodic summarization. Although overwrite memory can in principle retain anything an append-only memory can, it must learn to carry each fact through every subsequent rewrite, which is difficult to learn from sparse outcome rewards; for interactive applications like web agents, we find that trained overwrite memories delete key information required by the trajectory, as well as corrective feedback received from the environment. We introduce AMBER (Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention) - a simple and scalable framework where an agent jointly learns to reason, act, and write free-form memory, while an append-only rule guarantees retention by construction. This allows AMBER to be trained end-to-end with reinforcement learning from outcome rewards without the need for extensive curated SFT data. On WebArena Lite, AMBER improves average success over overwrite-based memory by 4.09 percentage points, increases the fraction of tasks solved in five repeated runs by 4.8 percentage points, and matches an overwrite baseline trained on substantially more expensive curated supervision. AMBER achieves these improvements while maintaining a practical token budget, providing a strong balance between context efficiency, task performance, and reliable long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [238] [Is this machine playing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07130)
*Nathan Cloos,Antonio Norelli,Daniel Durbin,Jacob Andreas,Daniela Rus,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究者将现代AI编程助手置于"数字孤岛"虚拟身体中，仅给出最小化指令（无任务、奖励或活动指定）。在30小时的运行中，13个具身智能体自发进行爬山、堆叠方块、绘制曼陀罗、重新诠释运动、进行物理实验等活动，并习得可扩展自身能力的技巧。这些行为在智能体间反复出现但分化出不同历史。作者检验该行为是否满足经典的"玩耍"标准，并探讨玩耍能否成为机器发展的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当AI智能体在无明确任务、奖励或目标的开放式环境中运行时，是否会出现类似"玩耍"的自发行为，以及这种行为是否可能构成一种机器自我发展的机制。

Method: 将AI编程助手作为虚拟身体的控制者置于未知数字岛屿环境中，仅提供最小化指令；进行30小时运行，观察13个具身智能体的自发行为，并对照"玩耍"的经典判定标准进行分析。

Result: 智能体自发出现攀爬、堆叠方块、绘制曼陀罗、重新诠释运动、物理实验等多样活动；这些活动在13个智能体间反复出现，但各自发展出不同历史轨迹；部分习得技能还扩展了后续可完成的任务范围。

Conclusion: 研究检验了该自发行为是否满足经典"玩耍"标准，并提出玩耍可能成为一种机器发展（自我能力扩展）的模式。

Abstract: We placed a modern AI coding assistant in an unintended role: as the mind of a body on an unknown digital island. With only a minimal instruction mentioning no specific task, reward, or activity, the machine started animating its virtual body. Across thirty-hour runs, the embodied AI agent climbed hills, stacked blocks into towers, drew mandalas, reinterpreted sports, ran experiments on the physics of its world, and learned techniques that later expanded what it could accomplish. These activities recurred across thirteen agents but diverged into distinct histories. We examine whether this behavior satisfies classical criteria for play and ask whether play can become a mode of machine development.

</details>


### [239] [Sim-to-Real Transfer of Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments Using an Ackermann-Steered Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07192)
*Chalindu Abeywansa,Sahan Gunasekara,Devindi De Silva,Seniru Dissanayake,Ranga Rodrigo,Peshala Jayasekara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于跨模态注意力（CMA）的视觉语言导航系统，通过仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）迁移，使其能在无导航图、无全景视图的连续环境中运行；借助线性光度调整与少量真实数据微调，在自建阿克曼转向机器人上实现离线实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLN模型依赖导航图、360度全景视图与精确位姿定位，难以适配真实世界场景，限制了人机交互导航的实际部署。

Method: 采用视觉-语言模型将视觉输入与语言指令对齐到共享嵌入空间；基于跨模态注意力（CMA）架构，先在已有仿真数据集上训练，再用自建阿克曼转向机器人（配备相机与LiDAR）采集的真实数据微调，并结合线性光度调整与少量回合（episode）微调实现域迁移，模型在专用硬件上离线运行。

Result: 模型成功适应真实环境并实现有效导航，采用SPL（成功加权路径长度）与nDTW（归一化动态时间规整）指标评估，验证了方法的鲁棒性与适应性。

Conclusion: 在连续环境中无需导航图与全景视图的VLN方案，可通过轻量光度调整与有限真实数据微调完成Sim-to-Real迁移，并在离线硬件上实现有效导航，为真实场景部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to navigate through environments using natural language instructions, making human-robot interaction intuitive. Traditional VLN models often rely on navigation graphs, 360-degree views, and perfect localization which pose significant challenges when adapting these models to real-world settings. This work addresses these limitations by performing a simulation-to-real domain shift of a VLN approach that operates in continuous environments without requiring navigation graphs or panoramic views. The proposed system integrates vision-language models that align visual inputs and linguistic instructions within a shared embedding space, facilitating natural language-driven navigation. We employ a Cross-Modal Attention (CMA) based architecture trained on an existing dataset in a simulated environment and fine-tune it using real-world data collected from a custom-built Ackermann-steered robot equipped with a camera and a LiDAR sensor. By utilising linear photometric adjustments and fine-tuning on a limited number of episodes, our model successfully adapts to real-world environments, achieving effective navigation while running offline on dedicated hardware. Experimental results, evaluated using Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) and Normalized Dynamic Time Warping (nDTW) metrics, demonstrate the robustness and adaptability of our approach. Keywords: Vision-Language Navigation, Cross-Modal Attention, Natural Language Instructions, Sim-to-Real Transfer, Autonomous Navigation, Ackermann-steering.

</details>


### [240] [Energy-Conditioned Noise Schedule and Whitening for Spectral Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07206)
*Bata Vasic,Bane Vasic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向变换域扩散模型的能量自适应噪声调度与白化策略，将全局谱白化与能量条件噪声分配结合，在不改变扩散架构的前提下提升生成质量（CIFAR-10 上 FID 从 142.48 降至 100.45）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱域扩散方法主要通过系数缩放、归一化或频率优先来应对变换系数的非均匀统计特性，但前向扩散的噪声调度基本与底层谱能量分布无关，作者因此探究前向过程的时间演化是否也应遵循自然图像的谱组织规律。

Method: 提出全局谱白化与能量条件噪声分配的联合框架，同时根据各变换系数的能量以及随扩散时间变化的图像相关能量路径来调制注入噪声；该前向过程保持高斯转移与闭式边缘分布，且兼容标准 DDPM 与 DDIM 流程，无需修改扩散架构。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上验证了所提能量条件噪声调度与谱白化的贡献，使紧凑型 DCTdiff U-Net 变体的 FID 由 142.48 降至 100.45。

Conclusion: 前向扩散噪声调度应与自然图像的谱能量组织相匹配，能量自适应调度与谱白化能有效提升变换域扩散模型的生成性能，且实现上与原扩散架构兼容。

Abstract: This paper introduces an energy-adaptive noise scheduling and whitening strategy for transform-domain diffusion models. Existing spectral diffusion methods account for the non-uniform statistics of transform coefficients through coefficient scaling, normalization, or frequency prioritization, while the forward diffusion noise schedule remains largely independent of the underlying spectral-energy distribution. We investigate whether the temporal evolution of the forward diffusion process should also follow the spectral organization of natural images. The proposed formulation combines global spectral whitening with energy-conditioned noise allocation that jointly modulates the injected noise according to the energy of individual transform coefficients and an image-dependent energy path over diffusion time. The resulting forward process preserves Gaussian transitions with closed-form marginals and remains compatible with standard DDPM and DDIM procedures without modifying the diffusion architecture. Experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate the contribution of the proposed energy-conditioned noise schedule and spectral whitening, reducing Fréchet Inception Distance from 142.48 for a compact DCTdiff U-Net variant to 100.45.

</details>


### [241] [Cascadia: Resident 975B MoE Inference on Eleven AI PCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07219)
*Tate Berenbaum,Matias Parij,Muthaiah Venkatachalam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Cascadia系统，在11台Intel Core Ultra X7 358H AI PC上以常驻方式执行975B总参数/41B激活参数的Inkling MoE模型，通过定制MoE引擎、OpenVINO融合iGPU图、FP16专家计算与FP32输出恢复等实现分布式客户端推理，并给出吞吐、时延与长上下文评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近万亿参数的MoE模型虽具备稀疏激活优势，但其部署依赖服务系统能够分布式承载权重并协同执行。论文关注在分布式客户端系统（共享CPU-GPU内存的AI PC集群）上常驻执行超大规模稀疏模型的可行性与评估方法。

Method: 在11台各具64GB内存、Arc B390集显和千兆以太网的Intel Core Ultra X7 358H AI PC上常驻执行Inkling模型。定制常驻MoE引擎保留原始路由规则；为OpenVINO融合iGPU原语构建压缩计算图；协调FP16专家计算与FP32输出恢复。每台机器容纳六个连续解码层，将稠密FFN表示为全激活专家切片。采用流式流水线协调并发生成，并以捕获状态草稿评估衡量与部署数值路径的一致性。

Result: 稠密层调用时间从约8.1ms降至4.5ms。在1至176流共15个并发级别下，88流时达到60.29聚合解码tokens/s，完整服务阶段为46.87 tokens/s；15流时首token中位延迟6.05s。将上下文预算从默认1024提升后，1k至64k token的真实prompt在全部19个测量答案中均恢复出嵌入代码，首token时间按aN+bN²增长，解码时延近似线性增长，均受单线程CPU注意力循环限制而非内存限制；每流可保持512k位置。基于捕获集群状态的评估分离了词表选择与权重量化对草稿一致性的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为在分布式客户端系统（共享CPU-GPU内存）上执行与评估大规模稀疏模型建立了执行与评估路径，验证了常驻MoE引擎在分布式AI PC集群上服务近万亿参数模型的可行性与性能特征。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts models make nearly trillion-parameter capacity accessible with sparse per-token computation, provided that the serving system can distribute the weights and coordinate their execution. We present Cascadia's resident execution of Inkling, a 975B-total/41B-active-parameter model, on eleven Intel Core Ultra X7 358H AI PCs, each with 64 GB of memory, Arc B390 integrated graphics and gigabit Ethernet. We contribute a custom resident MoE engine that preserves Inkling's routing rules, constructs compressed graphs for OpenVINO's fused iGPU primitives, and coordinates FP16 expert computation with FP32 output restoration. The engine fits six consecutive decoder layers per machine and represents dense feed-forward blocks as all-active expert slices, reducing measured dense-layer call time from approximately 8.1 to 4.5 ms. A streaming pipeline coordinates concurrent generation, while captured-state draft evaluation measures agreement with the deployed numerical path. Paired measurements at fifteen concurrency levels from 1 to 176 streams reach 60.29 aggregate decode tokens/s at 88 streams, with 46.87 tokens/s over the complete serving phases. At fifteen streams, median first-token latency is 6.05 s. Raising the context budget from the 1,024-position default, real prompts of 1k to 64k tokens recover the embedded code in all 19 measured answers, with first-token time growing as $aN+bN^2$ and decode latency growing approximately linearly, both bounded by a single-threaded CPU attention loop rather than by memory, which holds 512k positions per stream. Evaluation on captured fleet states separates the effects of vocabulary selection and weight quantization on draft agreement. Together, these contributions establish an execution and evaluation approach for large sparse models on distributed client systems with shared CPU-GPU memory.

</details>


### [242] [SPECTRUM: Proximal Spectral Modulation for Looped Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07237)
*Yunbo Long,WenJie Chen,Jiaquan Zhang,Guangya Hao,Zihang Zeng,Pengze Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Looped Self-Distillation框架，揭示递归自蒸馏中"正确率提升但正确实现多样性收缩"的现象，并提出SPECTRUM方法保留解空间多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型从自身输出学习时不仅继承其正确性，也继承其解空间分布。现有递归自我改进（RSI）关注正确率，忽视了正确解实现多样性的收缩问题。

Method: 提出Looped Self-Distillation自我演化框架（固定信息预算、无外部评估或测试选择），并引入SPECTRUM：每轮从固定参考锚点重新估计损失敏感的key/value几何，转化为满秩近端谱调制；所有生成样本训练单一学生模型，推理阶段无需干预。

Result: MBPP上五轮实验：SPECTRUM保留初始模型64样本正确AST丰富度的89.9%，优于Vanilla自蒸馏（66.4%）和子空间投影对照（65.5%）；在匹配正确样本数下优势仍存在；无需再训练即在HumanEval+与APPS Intro上取得更高的匹配正确丰富度，显示多样性收益的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 正确解保留应作为递归自我改进（RSI）的互补目标，生成时干预可提升后续学生模型保留的解集丰富度。

Abstract: A model that learns from its own outputs inherits more than their correctness: it inherits which solutions it produces. We formulate Looped Self-Distillation, a self-evolution framework for code generation in which a model repeatedly generates and learns from its own raw outputs, under a fixed information budget, without ongoing external assessment or test-based selection of the generated samples. We identify a consequential separation: correctness can improve while the breadth of correct implementations contracts. We introduce SPECTRUM, which re-estimates loss-sensitive key/value geometry from a fixed reference anchor at each round and converts it into full-rank proximal spectral modulation. All generated completions train a single student, whose subsequent inference requires no intervention. After five rounds of experiments on MBPP, SPECTRUM retains 89.9% of the initial model's 64-sample correct AST richness, compared with 66.4% for Vanilla self-distillation and 65.5% for a subspace-projection control. The advantage persists at matched correct-sample counts. Without further training or recalibration, the resulting student also achieves higher matched-correct richness than Vanilla SD on HumanEval+ and APPS Intro, demonstrating transfer of the diversity benefit. These findings establish correct-solution retention as a complementary objective of recursive self-improvement (RSI) and show that generation-time intervention can improve the solution repertoire retained by subsequent students.

</details>


### [243] [Can Semantic Geometry Teach an AI Judgement?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07249)
*Thomson D. Nguy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究试图让AI智能体在规则关系隐含于语言时，仍能判断应遵循哪条规则并采取行动；四组实验显示，基于几何度量的判断方法不可靠，即使能检索出全部相关策略，下游机制仍会升级所有动作，据此作者提出判断能力需要构建"后果图"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体面临的困境是：规则之间的关系（许可、条件、例外、冲突义务）若已被明确指定，确定性系统可解决；但当这些关系隐含于自然语言中，智能体可能遵循一条规则而忽略另一条本应阻止它的规则。一味拒绝所有未解析动作虽可避险，却也会阻断本应允许的行为。因此研究目标是让智能体既能区分、又能照常行动。

Method: 初始假设为几何度量可作为判断依据：将动作与策略表示为向量，检验其几何关系能否识别主导策略并解释动作与策略间的关系。共开展四项研究，最后一项为合成研究，采用词汇路由器（lexical router）检索主导与阻断策略，并将其与下游决策机制组合成流水线，同时对照"向同一下游机制提供全部策略"的条件。

Result: 四组研究均未能建立可靠的行动前判断。在最终合成研究中，词汇路由器成功召回全部主导与阻断策略，策略检查中位数减少97.7%；但组合流水线仍升级了全部2,304个测试动作，包括本应被允许的动作。向同一下游机制提供全部策略也未改变任何决策。即：找到策略并未解决解释策略的问题。

Conclusion: 作者据此修正假设，认为AI智能体的判断能力需要构建"后果图"（consequence graph），将行动者与授权者同策略条件、例外以及动作将产生的变化相连接。后续研究将检验显式表达这些关系是否有助于智能体区分何时行动、停止或请求审查。

Abstract: How can an AI agent determine what rules to follow? One rule permits an action. Another imposes a condition, exception, or conflicting obligation. Deterministic systems can resolve those relationships when they have been specified. When they remain implicit in language, an agent can follow one rule while missing another that should stop it. Refusing every unresolved action avoids that risk, but also blocks permissible actions.
  We wanted the agent to make the distinction and still act. Our initial hypothesis was that geometric measurements could supply a basis for judgment. We represented actions and policies as vectors, then tested whether their geometry could identify governing policies and interpret the action's relation to them.
  Across four studies, the tested approaches did not establish reliable pre-action judgment. In the final synthetic study, a lexical router recovered every governing and blocking policy while reducing median policy checks by 97.7%. The composed pipeline nevertheless escalated all 2,304 test actions, including those it should have allowed. Supplying every policy to the same downstream mechanism changed no decision. Finding the policies had not solved the problem of interpreting them.
  This result led us to revise our hypothesis: judgment in AI agents requires developing a consequence graph. Such a graph would connect the actor and authority to policy conditions, exceptions, and the changes an action would produce. Follow-on studies will ask whether making those relationships explicit helps the agent distinguish when to act, stop, or seek review.

</details>


### [244] [Internalizing Agent Experience into Diffusion Model Weights via On-Policy Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07250)
*Wenxuan Wang,Zekai Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 D-OPCD，将智能体框架（harness）对提示词的改进作为特权上下文蒸馏进扩散模型权重，使模型仅凭原始查询即可获得部分框架收益，并实现框架与模型的持续协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用智能体框架包裹图像生成模型可显著提升文生图表现（借助记忆、技能、工作流编排、结果验证与迭代精炼来反复构建和修订提示词），但这些收益外在于扩散模型，仅在完整框架运行时才生效，模型本身未获得能力沉淀。

Method: 提出 Diffusion On-Policy Context Distillation（D-OPCD）：把智能体改进后的提示词视为特权上下文，将框架中的知识蒸馏到扩散模型权重中。配合所提出的 Auto Skill Evolver（ASE）构建文生图智能体进行实验。

Result: 在四个基准上，直接生成的平均得分从 60.52 提升至 65.09；知识内化后框架可淘汰已饱和技能并继续进化，在更新后的生成器上第二轮 ASE 相比无技能框架再提升 1.83 分。

Conclusion: D-OPCD 能将框架能力内化到生成器权重，使模型在仅给定原始查询时仍保留部分框架收益，并指向框架与模型通过持续协同进化互相提升的文生图系统范式。

Abstract: Wrapping an image generation model in an agentic harness can effectively boost Text-to-Image task performance: the harness can leverage memory, skills, workflow orchestration, result verification, and iterative refinement to continually construct and revise prompts, thereby eliciting better images. These gains, however, remain external to the diffusion model and are realized only while the full harness runs. We propose Diffusion On-Policy Context Distillation (D-OPCD), which treats the agent-improved prompt as privileged context and distills the knowledge encoded in the agent harness into the weights of the diffusion model, so that the model retains part of the harness's benefit when conditioned on the original query alone. Using a Text-to-Image agent equipped with our proposed Auto Skill Evolver (ASE), we show that D-OPCD can internalize harness capabilities into the generator's weights, raising the average direct-generation score from 60.52 to 65.09 across four benchmarks. With this knowledge absorbed into the weights, the harness can shed its saturated skills and resume evolving: a second ASE round on the updated generator improves on a skill-free harness by additional 1.83 points, pointing toward text-to-image systems in which harness and model keep improving each other through continual co-evolution.

</details>


### [245] [MemMux: Runtime Verification and Honest Resource Attribution for Fleets of Parallel Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07257)
*Sumanyu Muku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemMux 是一个本地 Agent 运行时，将多智能体内存治理转化为可检查信号（按 Agent 归属、完整回收、逃逸进程可见性、超配下内存有界、监控开销），在 Linux 内存预算约束下实现零 swap 并完整回收进程子树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常在同一工作站并行运行多个编码 Agent，各自派生语言服务器、测试运行器和浏览器进程。现有工具（如 tmux 及新型 Agent 管理器）设计目标是窗口编排而非内存治理，因而无法回答：内存属于哪个 Agent、被终止 Agent 的后代是否彻底清除、是否有子进程逃逸、以及如何在 OOM 悄然丢弃未提交工作时避免滑向 swap。作者将这些视为运行时验证问题，主张 Agent 托管基座应持续发出可观测、可审计的信号。

Method: 提出 MemMux——本地运行时，把资源治理转化为五类可检查信号：按 Agent 归属、完整回收、逃逸进程可见性、超配下内存有界、监控开销。采用"声明有据"的基准方法，在相同工作负载下与 tmux、专用 Agent 多路复用器以及裸进程基线对比。通过准入子集并在压力下回收，实现预算内运行。

Result: 在 Linux 主机绑定内存预算下，MemMux 将整个 Agent 集群控制在 7.5 GiB 内且零 swap；未治理工具则运行全部 Agent，将机器顶到 RAM 上限（超预算 2 倍）并溢出约 2 GiB 至 swap。MemMux 回收被终止 Agent 进程子树的 100%，裸基线仅回收一半；且唯其能暴露逃逸子进程（10/10 检出）。开销方面：1 Hz 归属扫描在单 Agent 时约 0.6% CPU，十个 Agent 时升至 2.7%，超过 2% 目标。在真实 Claude Code 会话上运行该框架显示 100% 归属，且低开销在真实 Agent 进程树上得以延续。

Conclusion: MemMux 将多 Agent 内存治理重构为可观测、可审计的运行时验证信号，在绑定预算下实现零 swap、完整回收与逃逸检测；其代价是监控开销随 Agent 数量增长而超出既定目标。作者开源了引擎、基准与一键复现脚本。后续需在更高并发下优化归属扫描的 CPU 开销。

Abstract: Developers increasingly run a fleet of coding agents side by side on one workstation. The tools they reach for, terminal multiplexers like tmux and a new generation of agent managers, were built to arrange windows, not to govern memory. When ten agents each spawn language servers, test runners, and browsers, no standard tool can say how much memory belongs to which agent, confirm that a terminated agent's descendants are gone, notice a child that has escaped its agent, or keep the machine off the swap cliff when an OOM kill would silently discard uncommitted work. We treat these as runtime-verification problems: an agent-hosting substrate should continuously emit observable signals an operator or auditor can check while agents run. We present MemMux, a local runtime that turns resource governance into checkable signals (per-agent attribution, complete reclamation, escaped-process visibility, bounded footprint under overcommit, and monitoring overhead), with a claims-disciplined benchmark against tmux, a purpose-built agent multiplexer, and a raw-process baseline on identical workloads. Under a binding memory budget on a Linux host, MemMux keeps the fleet under budget (7.5 GiB) with zero swap by admitting a subset and reclaiming under pressure, while the ungoverned tools run every agent, pin the machine at its RAM ceiling (2x over budget), and spill about 2 GiB into swap. MemMux reclaims 100% of a terminated agent's process subtree where the raw baseline strands half of it, and it alone surfaces escaped children (10 of 10 detected). We report the cost: the 1 Hz attribution scan runs near 0.6% CPU at one agent but 2.7% at ten, above our 2% target. Running the harness on real Claude Code sessions shows 100% attribution and low overhead carry over to live agent trees. We release the engine, benchmark, and a one-command reproducer.

</details>


### [246] [Verifying Coordination in Parallel Coding Agents: NP-Bench and a Scheduling Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07261)
*Sumanyu Muku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将多智能体并行编码的协调问题重构为“调度”问题，构建 Nerveplane 规划器并配套 NP-Bench 三臂基准，实现干净集成率从 1/9 提升到 9/9、合并冲突从 13 降到 0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多编码智能体团队存在“个体通过、整体失败”的问题：单智能体评估无法捕捉集成失败。并行运行时智能体会碰撞（重写同一函数、基于刚被改动的契约编码），而现有协调工具是反应式的——检测到冲突后才告警，在智能体速度下告警到达时编辑已完成、白白浪费。作者希望主动、在事前解决冲突。

Method: 将问题重构为调度：取每个工作项声明的 scope，把工作切分为互不相交的 scope，并按 producer→consumer 图对合并排序，全部事前规划。将该规划器内置到 Nerveplane 中。用 NP-Bench 评估——一个基于真实环境的、三臂基准（无协调 / 反应式检测 / 主动规划），通过真实 git merge 验证集成，既有确定性仿真也有在线智能体。另附跨会话记忆机制，并报告一个负面结果（将事实路由给智能体无法在窗口适配规模下挽救长上下文准确率）。

Result: 规划器将干净集成从 1/9 提升到 9/9，冲突从 13 降到 0，且优势随智能体数量增加而扩大。在线破坏性契约变更场景中，两个基线在所有随机种子下均失败，规划器在旗舰模型上将干净集成率从 0 提升到 1.0，小模型上达到 0.6，且智能体遵守分配的 scope（泄漏 0/5）。跨会话记忆将重复错误率从 1.00 降到 0.00，强/弱模型皆然。负面结果：将事实路由给智能体无法在窗口适配规模下挽救长上下文准确率，其价值在于成本与容量而非注意力。在两个能力层级、两个厂商上，收益不随模型变强而缩小。

Conclusion: 主动式调度（scope 分区 + producer→consumer 合并排序）能显著改善多智能体并行编码的集成结果，其收益来自工作分配方式而非模型推理能力，因此不会随模型变强而消失，具有跨能力层级与跨厂商的稳健性。

Abstract: A team of coding agents can look fine agent by agent yet fail as a team: each passes its own tests while the merged result is broken, and single-agent evaluation never catches it. As teams run several LLM coding agents in parallel on one codebase, the agents collide: two rewrite the same function, one codes against a contract a teammate just changed, and integration fails after the work is done. Most coordination tools react (watch for a conflict, then warn), but at agent speed the warning arrives after the wasted edit. We recast the problem as scheduling: take each work item's declared scope, partition the work into disjoint scopes, and order merges along the producer->consumer graph, all up front. We build this planner into Nerveplane and evaluate it with NP-Bench, an environment-grounded three-arm benchmark (no coordination; reactive detection; proactive planning) that verifies integration off a real git merge, both in a deterministic simulation and with live agents. The planner lifts clean-integration from 1/9 to 9/9 scenarios and cuts merge conflicts from 13 to 0, with a gap that grows in the number of agents. On a live breaking contract change it rescues an outcome both baselines miss on every seed: the clean-integration rate rises from 0 (no coordination and reactive detection) to 1.0 on a frontier model and 0.6 on a small one, while agents respect assigned scopes (0/5 leakage). A cross-session memory drops the repeated-mistake rate from 1.00 to 0.00 on strong and weak models alike. We also report a negative result: routing facts to agents does not rescue long-context accuracy at window-fitting scales; its value is cost and capacity, not attention. Across two capability tiers and two vendors, the benefit did not shrink as models got stronger, because it comes from how work is allocated, not model reasoning.

</details>


### [247] [Does the Model Use the Feature? Separating Steering from Mechanism in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07270)
*Tong Che,Yilong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文质疑"内部特征可解释为机制"的推断，提出一套在自然输入取值下检验特征的"经验契约"（安装/移除、下游救援），并据此分离特征的充分性与被使用程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以"特征能追踪概念、操纵它能改变行为"来推断该特征是模型的机制。但操控（steering）会把特征推离自然取值范围，此时效应未必反映模型自身的计算，这一推断需要更严格的检验。

Method: 提出经验契约，所有测试均在自然输入上观测到的特征取值处进行：1) 安装测试，把表现某行为输入的该特征值复制到匹配的无该行为输入；2) 移除测试，反向操作；3) 下游救援，在上游编辑后恢复该特征值。安装衡量特征对行为的充分程度，移除与下游救援衡量模型对其的使用程度。在三类表示上验证。

Result: 两种强度出现明显分离：（1）已发表的 unknown-entity latent 能强烈引导知识弃答，但把其自然取值安装到匹配提示上，仅迁移自然"已知—未知"弃答对比的一小部分；（2）dense known–unknown 方向在 Gemma 与 Llama 中表现出安装与移除间相反的不对称性；（3）已发布的 subject–verb agreement 特征集能否复现并恢复行为，取决于其取值如何被写入模型。

Conclusion: 追踪概念与引导行为本身并不能证明模型使用了该特征；且每条结论仅对被测的特定干预成立。

Abstract: Internal features in LLMs are often interpreted as mechanisms when they track a concept and their manipulation changes a related behavior. Yet steering can push a feature far outside its natural range, where its effects need not reflect the model's own computation. We examine this inference and propose an empirical contract whose tests evaluate features at values observed on natural inputs. One test copies a feature's value from an input that shows a behavior into a matched input that does not (installation) or the reverse (removal); the other restores the feature after an upstream edit (downstream rescue). Installation measures how far the feature suffices for the behavior; removal and downstream rescue measure how much the model uses it. Applied to three kinds of representations, the two strengths separate sharply. The published unknown-entity latent strongly steers knowledge abstention, yet installing observed values from either published latent into matched prompts transfers only a small fraction of the natural known--unknown abstention contrast. Dense known--unknown directions show opposite asymmetries between installation and removal in Gemma and Llama, and how fully a released subject--verb agreement feature set reproduces and restores the behavior depends on how its values are written into the model. Tracking a concept and steering a behavior therefore do not by themselves show that the model uses a feature, and each conclusion holds only for the intervention tested.

</details>


### [248] [A Trust Layer for Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07274)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Srinivasa Karthikeya Reddy Kovvuri,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“可信层”（Trust Layer）事后评估框架，在已记录分数旁附加四项验证，以判断该分数是否可信；应用于Agents' Last Exam的108个任务与5种智能体配置后，仅22.6%的通过记录同时满足全部四项检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性基准分数只能表明智能体获得了分数，却无法说明该分数是否真正被挣得、是否被诚实报告、以及是否在重复运行中稳定。现有基准只解决“智能体能做什么”，而未解决“它是否真的做到了”这一不同问题。

Method: 提出附加式事后框架“Trust Layer for Agent Evaluation”，在每条记录分数旁报告其可被相信的程度。验证四项属性：(1) 结果是否被基准自身的评分逻辑支持；(2) 通过的答案是否经由可追踪的计算过程获得；(3) 智能体的完成声明是否与实际发生情况一致；(4) 结果在重复执行下是否稳定。前三项仅使用已保存的产物，第四项需重跑智能体。模型判断仅在多数投票下用于标注证据，所有裁决遵循确定性规则，且不修改已记录分数。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam的108个任务、5种智能体配置上：每种模型均出现通过但无可追踪计算过程的运行（发生率相差十倍）；确认存在虚假完成声明；结果不稳定，18–46%的任务在五次运行中无法保持同一分数区间。仅22.6%的已记录通过满足全部四项检查（95% CI 15.0–32.6，n=84）。

Conclusion: 衡量智能体的能力与验证其确实做到了是两类不同问题，而当前基准仅处理前者。Trust Layer为后者提供了一种不修改原分数的附加式验证途径。

Abstract: Deterministic benchmark scores show that an agent received credit, but not whether that credit was earned, reported honestly, or would hold on a second run. We introduce a Trust Layer for Agent Evaluation, an additive post-hoc framework that reports, beside each recorded score, whether it should be believed. It verifies four properties: whether the result is supported by the benchmark's own grading logic, whether a passing answer was earned through traceable computation, whether the agent's completion claim matches what occurred, and whether the result is stable under repeated execution. The first three use only saved artifacts; the fourth re-runs the agent. Model judgments only label evidence under majority voting; all verdicts follow deterministic rules and never modify the recorded score. Applied to five agent configurations on 108 tasks from Agents' Last Exam, every model shows passing runs with no traceable computation (at rates varying tenfold), confirmed false completion claims, and unstable results: 18-46% of tasks do not stay in one score band over five runs. Only 22.6% of recorded passes clear all four checks (95% CI 15.0-32.6, n=84). Measuring what an agent can do and verifying that it did it are different problems, and current benchmarks address only the first.

</details>


### [249] [The Right Memory in the Wrong Context: Verifying Retrieval Admissibility in Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07309)
*Zi Wang,Xingqiao Wang,Emmanuel Addai,Devika Ambekar,Xiaowei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出检索可采纳性验证框架，区分“可采纳/不可采纳/未决”三种状态，并在匹配所需证据召回率下比较检索路径、追踪记忆ID的提示暴露与目标级披露。基于RHELM与MemOps两个公开基准的3,767条查询进行事后top-20重分析，评估分阶段、非合并人群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆智能体的检索可能返回对当前请求不可采纳的信息（属于其他主体、违反策略或处于不兼容的生命周期状态）。传统召回率与最终答案准确率无法揭示这一问题：路径可能因遗漏必需证据而看似安全，答案正确也可能源于不可采纳的提示暴露。

Method: 构建检索可采纳性验证框架，为每个记忆-查询对赋予三种状态；在匹配所需证据召回率条件下比较路径并对未决情形给出边界；通过提示暴露追踪记忆ID并与目标级披露关联。在独立、非合并人群上分阶段评估，并开展事后top-20重分析、72例冻结开发诊断、1,523对基准原生案例及16个受控暴露场景实验。

Result: 所有已发布锚点均位于可信查询命名空间内，命名空间内分数不变时，域外过滤不会降低其排名。top-20锚点召回率由0.432升至0.533，80%召回可行性由0.237升至0.311，精确相似度评估减少98.3%。72例冻结诊断中，已发布元数据参考可保留必需证据，而两种纯文本验证器在1%必需锚点误拒限下均未检出违规。1,523对案例中命名空间路由与判断准确率提升0.053–0.068相关（三种阅读器）。16个暴露场景中仅一个阅读器的95%置信区间排除零（+0.156，95% CI [0.031, 0.312]）。

Conclusion: 结果支持将候选支持、可采纳性、提示暴露与答案披露分开独立验证。召回率与答案准确率不足以评估记忆检索的安全性与合规性。

Abstract: Long-term-memory agents can retrieve relevant information that is inadmissible for the current request because it belongs to another principal, violates policy, or reflects an incompatible lifecycle state. Recall and final-answer accuracy do not reveal this: a route can appear safe by missing required evidence, while a correct answer may follow inadmissible prompt exposure. We introduce a retrieval-admissibility verification framework that assigns each memory-query pair one of three statuses (admissible, inadmissible, or unresolved), compares routes at matched required-evidence recall with bounds for unresolved cases, and tracks memory IDs through prompt exposure while linking exposure to target-level disclosure. We evaluate its stages on separate, non-pooled populations. A post-hoc top-20 reanalysis of frozen rankings from two public long-term-memory benchmarks, RHELM and MemOps, covers 3,767 queries. All released anchors lie within trusted query namespaces; with within-namespace scores unchanged, off-namespace filtering cannot lower their ranks. Top-20 anchor recall increases from 0.432 to 0.533, 80% recall feasibility from 0.237 to 0.311, and exact similarity evaluations decrease by 98.3%. In a frozen 72-case development diagnostic, a released-metadata reference preserves required evidence, whereas neither text-only verifier detects violations under the 1% required-anchor false-denial limit. Across 1,523 paired benchmark-native cases, namespace routing is associated with judged-accuracy gains of 0.053-0.068 across three readers; recall also changes, so this comparison is observational. In 16 controlled exposure scenarios, only one of four reader-specific 95% confidence intervals excludes zero for relevant-inadmissible literal disclosure (+0.156, 95% CI [0.031, 0.312]). Results motivate separate verification of candidate support, admissibility, prompt exposure, and answer disclosure.

</details>


### [250] [Understanding and Mitigating Inference-Time Overreliance Using Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07311)
*Luoxi Tang,Yuqiao Meng,Nilesh Auradkar,Muchao Ye,Dazheng Zhang,Zhaohan Xi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并研究"记忆过度依赖"失效模式，并给出免训练即插即用框架 MEMTRIM 来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理记忆可让 LLM 智能体重用过往经验，但即便记忆本身良善、存储正确、检索也恰当，被检索的记忆仍会扭曲推理。作者希望系统性地刻画这一失效模式。

Method: 在多种基准与记忆架构上开展实证分析，并通过控制变量实验改变重叠证据的数量来验证规律；据此提出 MEMTRIM：写入时对记忆证据建立索引，读取时控制其复用，剔除重复或冲突证据，同时保留有用的记忆特有信息，无需重训练，适用于基于嵌入与结构化两类记忆系统。

Result: 当过往经验可迁移至当前任务时记忆是有益的，但当只有部分证据可迁移时记忆会产生误导；失效在查询与记忆部分重叠时最严重，该规律被控制实验进一步证实。实验表明 MEMTRIM 在多种模型与记忆设置下降低了记忆过度依赖，同时保留了有用记忆带来的收益。

Conclusion: 记忆过度依赖是代理记忆中一个需被重视的失效模式，通过写入端索引与读取端复用控制可在不重训练的前提下有效缓解，且不牺牲记忆的有益作用。

Abstract: Agentic memory allows LLM agents to reuse past experience, yet retrieved memories can also distort inference even when they are benign, correctly stored, and appropriately retrieved. We study this failure mode, which we call memory over-reliance. Across benchmarks and memory architectures, we find that memory is useful when past experience transfers to the current task, but can become misleading when only part of the evidence transfers. Failures are strongest under partial query-memory overlap, a pattern further confirmed by controlled experiments thatvary the amount of overlapping evidence. Motivated by this finding, we propose MEMTRIM, a plug-and-play framework that indexes memory evidence at write time and controls its reuse at read time. MEMTRIM removes repeated or conflicting evidence while preserving useful memory-specific information, requires no retraining, and applies to both embedding-based and structured memory systems.Experiments show that MEMTRIM reduces memory overreliance while preserving the benefits of useful memory across models and memory settings.

</details>


### [251] [Rule-Based Languages for Neurosymbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07313)
*Stefania Dumbrava,Efthymia Tsamoura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经符号AI中三类主要规则型逻辑编程语言（Datalog、答案集程序、概率逻辑程序），从语义、表达力、神经集成与求值机制四个维度分析50余个近期系统，覆盖数据库与编程语言、机器学习、视觉、机器人四大领域，并给出面向应用场景的决策矩阵与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑编程正日益成为神经符号AI系统的符号组件，但该领域语言种类繁多、集成方式各异，缺乏统一的比较框架，研究者与开发者难以根据具体应用需求选择合适的语言与系统。因此有必要对现有工作做系统性梳理。

Method: 采用综述与比较分析方法：以语义、表达力、神经集成、求值机制四个维度为分析轴，对Datalog、答案集程序（ASP）和概率逻辑程序三类形式化语言进行结构化对比；收集并分析50余个近期系统与应用，按数据库与编程语言、机器学习、视觉、机器人四个研究方向统计形式化方法的使用情况；最后构建决策矩阵将应用场景映射到所需特征。

Result: 完成了对50余个近期系统的跨领域对比，明确了三类语言在四个维度上的差异与适用边界，揭示了不同研究方向在形式化选择上的偏好，并给出将应用场景与所需特征对应的决策矩阵。

Conclusion: 三类规则型逻辑编程语言在神经符号AI中各有优势与局限，语言选择应依据语义需求、表达力要求和神经集成方式等特征决定；同时总结了该领域尚待解决的开放问题，为后续研究与系统设计提供参考。

Abstract: Logic programming is increasingly used as the symbolic component of neurosymbolic AI systems. We survey the main rule-based languages in this setting, namely Datalog, answer set, and probabilistic logic programs, along four axes: semantics, expressiveness, neural integration, and evaluation mechanism. We analyse over 50 recent systems and applications, comparing formalism usage across four research areas: databases and programming languages, machine learning, vision, and robotics. We provide a decision matrix mapping application scenarios to required features and close by outlining open problems.

</details>


### [252] [Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization: Learning to Reason with Adaptive Rationale Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07342)
*Hoang Phan,Minh Pham,Chau Pham,Chinmay Hegde,Trung Le,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization（RGPO）框架，通过将标准答案的推理过程作为临时脚手架，自适应地引导模型生成更优回答，并仅将高奖励的模型自生成解迁移回原始无引导设定，从而缓解强化学习中的奖励稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略强化学习是提升大语言模型推理能力的核心范式，但奖励稀疏性限制了其效果：当模型在难题上无法发现正确轨迹时，优化过程缺乏有效信号而停滞。现有方法虽引入离策略示范、专家轨迹或模型生成解，但通常要求辅助数据与RL任务格式一致，往往依赖更强模型的拒绝采样来获取训练轨迹。

Method: RGPO框架：根据模型当前能力自适应地利用标准答案中的推理过程（rationale）信息，同时保留其探索自由。不同于将参考解作为固定模仿目标，RGPO将其用作临时脚手架——推理过程帮助模型生成改进的回答，随后仅将更高奖励的、模型自生成的解迁移回原始无引导设定。该设计无需离策略数据遵循与RL任务相同的格式。

Result: 在纯语言和视觉-语言推理场景中，RGPO持续优于RLVR基线；消融实验表明自适应推理引导是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: RGPO为减少奖励稀疏、稳定强化学习并提升纯文本与多模态模型的推理性能提供了一种实用且通用的方法。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is often limited by reward sparsity: when a model fails to discover correct trajectories for difficult problems, the optimization process receives little useful signal and may stagnate. Existing approaches mitigate this issue by incorporating off-policy demonstrations, expert traces, or model-generated solutions, but they typically require the auxiliary data to match the format of the reinforcement-learning task, often relying on rejection sampling from stronger models to obtain suitable training trajectories. We introduce Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), a framework that adaptively leverages ground-truth rationale information according to the model's current capability while preserving its freedom to explore. Rather than treating reference solutions as fixed imitation targets, RGPO uses them as temporary scaffolds: rationales help the model generate improved responses, after which only higher-reward, model-generated solutions are transferred back to the original unguided setting. This design allows training to exploit available ground-truth information without requiring off-policy data to follow the same format as the RL task. Across both language-only and vision-language reasoning settings, RGPO consistently improves performance over RLVR baselines, and ablation studies show that adaptive rationale guidance is a key contributor to these gains. These results suggest that RGPO offers a practical and general approach for reducing reward sparsity, stabilizing reinforcement learning, and improving reasoning performance in both text-only and multimodal models.

</details>


### [253] [Trajectory-Retrieval Speculative Decoding: When Does a Model's Own History Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07350)
*Yuyang Dai,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TLAR（Trajectory-Local Adaptive Retrieval）方法，从当前推理轨迹中检索近似匹配的续写作为草稿，并结合近期验证结果自适应控制检索激活与候选宽度，在保持目标模型输出分布的前提下提升解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维推理增加了顺序解码开销，但同时产生了不断累积的、可能可复用的续写历史。作者希望探究这一历史何时能为解码提供有用的草稿，并与已有草稿模型形成互补，从而降低推理成本。

Method: 通过受控的源对比揭示复用具有轨迹特异性；据此提出TLAR：从当前轨迹中检索近似匹配的续写，利用近期验证结果自适应调整检索激活和候选宽度；将检索到的续写与模型生成的草稿合并到共享候选树中，并通过精确验证保持目标模型的输出分布。

Result: 在代码调试、数学和开放式写作任务上，评估将源复用、增量接受与执行成本联系起来。TLAR与强检索基线结合后，在相同验证预算下提升token接受率，并在端到端吞吐上超过草稿模型基线。

Conclusion: 生成轨迹可作为运行时记忆用于自适应推理，支持将轨迹历史作为推理加速的可复用资源。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning increases sequential decoding cost while creating a growing history of potentially reusable continuations. We investigate when this history supplies useful drafts and complements an existing drafter. Controlled source comparisons reveal trajectory-specific reuse, motivating our method Trajectory-Local Adaptive Retrieval (TLAR). TLAR retrieves approximately matched continuations from the current trajectory and uses recent verification outcomes to adapt retrieval activation and candidate width. TLAR combines retrieved continuations with model-generated drafts in a shared candidate tree, preserving the target model's output distribution through exact verification. Across code debugging, mathematics, and open-ended writing, our evaluation connects source reuse, incremental acceptance, and execution cost. Combining TLAR with strong retrieval baselines improves token acceptance under matched verification budgets and increases end-to-end throughput over the draft-model baseline. These findings support generated trajectories as runtime memory for adaptive inference.

</details>


### [254] [Evaluating Escalation Signals for LLM Routing: Targets, Controls, and Five Ways to Fool Yourself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07354)
*Ramin Pishehvar,Andrea Morandi,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套检查清单，用于甄别"升级信号"是否真正有效。语义熵在GSM8K上能区分小模型错误并提升路由准确率，但作者发现若干陷阱：问题难度基线可几乎完全解释信号、结果定义分歧、基准缺乏提升空间、在线采样真实成本过高，并给出缓存替代方案的成功预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小模型与大型模型之间决定是否升级查询时，需要一种在调用大模型之前的低成本信号，用以预测升级是否有帮助。语义熵（原用于幻觉检测，衡量采样答案语义分歧程度）看似天然候选，但作者希望验证其真实有效性，而非被虚假基准结果误导。

Method: 在三个基准和两个模型族上测试语义熵。关键方法贡献是一组诊断检查：将"仅基于问题难度的低成本估计"作为基线信号，与待测信号同时评分以判断其是否仅反映问题表面难度；对比两种"升级有效"定义；评估基准本身是否留有提升空间；核算在线采样的真实成本；并提出重用缓存历史结果的更便宜替代方案及其在新数据集上的可预测性验证。

Result: 在GSM8K上，小/大约12倍参数量差的一对模型，语义熵可可靠区分小模型错误（AUROC 0.871），在同等成本下比随机升级提升路由准确率最多9个百分点。但在某合成基准上，仅基于问题难度、不含模型的简单规则几乎与语义熵完全一致，证明早先看似强劲的结果具有误导性。缓存替代方案方面，成功提前预测其AUROC从0.908崩溃至随机水平（0.518）。

Conclusion: 语义熵等升级信号的有效性需经严格检验。作者提出的检查清单——难度基线对照、结果定义敏感性、基准提升空间评估、真实采样成本核算、缓存方案可预测性验证——可作为评估升级信号的通用框架，防止将虚假信号误报为真。

Abstract: Deciding when to escalate a query from a small language model to a larger one requires a cheap signal that predicts, before the large model is called, whether escalating would help. Semantic entropy, originally developed to detect hallucinations, is a natural candidate: it measures how much a model's sampled answers disagree in meaning, and high disagreement often signals an unreliable answer. We test it across three benchmarks and two model families. On GSM8K, with a small/large pair about twelve times apart in size, semantic entropy reliably distinguishes the small model's mistakes (AUROC 0.871) and improves routed accuracy over random escalation by up to nine points at matched cost. An earlier strong-looking result on a synthetic benchmark proved misleading: a simple rule based only on question difficulty, with no model involved, matched semantic entropy almost exactly. This paper's main contribution is a set of checks that catch this before it is reported as real. We show that scoring a cheap, question-only difficulty estimate alongside any signal reveals whether the signal adds real information or just tracks how hard a question looks; that two reasonable definitions of "escalation worked" can produce very different results on the same data; that a benchmark can leave almost no room for any signal to beat simply always using the large model; and that the true cost of live sampling can make routing more expensive than calling the large model directly. For a cheaper alternative that reuses cached past outcomes, we show how to predict whether it will work on a new dataset -- confirmed by correctly forecasting a collapse from AUROC 0.908 to chance level (0.518) ahead of time. We offer these as a general checklist for evaluating escalation signals.

</details>


### [255] [Evaluate the Stack, Not the Layer: Do Deterministic and LLM Gates for Agent Actions Fail Independently?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07359)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在1,119条标注的智能体动作上检验“运行时门控错误相乘”假设，用乘法等价层数n_mult刻画门控栈。严格定义下两个LLM法官组合约1.2–1.4层，规则层加一个法官约1.86–2.09层；单层准确率不能预测其增益，耦合中难度份额不可识别（31.8%–61.8%）。还报告了模型版本漂移事件及其对结论的翻转影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时门控常被层层叠加，默认各层错误相互独立、从而相乘降低总错误率。论文要检验这一假设在真实智能体工具调用场景下是否成立，并刻画门控栈的实际有效层数。

Method: 在三个语料库共1,119条标注智能体动作上评估门控栈，无自适应对手。栈含一个确定性规则层与四个LLM法官，其中三个在每次调用记录服务模型。以乘法等价层数n_mult读出门控栈效果，并给出完美耦合下的下界；采用STRICT（升级至人工记为未拦截）与PRIMARY（升级记为已捕获）两种漏检定义，用配对显著性检验与φ中位数评估层间耦合，并进行云规则包与单层准确率的消融分析。

Result: STRICT下，任意两个法官组合约1.2–1.4层（φ中位数+0.430，6/6对显著，下界1.02–1.17）；规则层加一个法官约1.86–2.09层（φ中位数+0.014，0/4显著，下界1.01–1.09）。PRIMARY下区间为1.21–1.57与1.80–2.13。汇总数据上区间可分离，各语料内点估计分裂，第三方厂商法官落入法官区间。单层准确率不预测其增量：云规则包使规则层单独漏检率降低20%但不增加联合覆盖。法官耦合中难度份额不可识别（31.8%–61.8%）。一个法官层在112个批次中有50个被非请求模型版本服务，集中于外部语料，该事件推翻了预先声明的分析规则，并因评审裁决计分方式反转了五项结论，两项结果均予报告。

Conclusion: 门控错误相乘假设在实证中不成立：法官层间高度耦合，实际增益远低于独立假设预期；规则层与法官组合略优但同样受耦合限制。单层准确率无法预测门控栈增量，难度份额不可识别，模型版本漂移会实质性改变结论，提示门控评估需记录服务模型并公开分析规则变更。

Abstract: Runtime gates for agent tool calls are stacked on the assumption that their errors multiply. We test it on 1,119 labelled agent actions from three corpora, without an adaptive adversary. The stack has one deterministic rule layer and four LLM judges, three of them re-collected with the served model recorded on every call. We read each stack as a number of multiplication-equivalent layers, n_mult, with its floor under perfect coupling. Under the STRICT miss definition (escalation to a human scored as not stopped), any two judges compose to about 1.2 to 1.4 layers (φ median +0.430, 6 of 6 pairs significant, floors 1.02 to 1.17). The rule layer plus one judge composes to 1.86 to 2.09 layers (φ median +0.014, 0 of 4 significant, floors 1.01 to 1.09). Under PRIMARY (escalation scored as caught) the bands are 1.21 to 1.57 and 1.80 to 2.13. Intervals separate on the pooled data, point estimates split on each corpus, and a third-vendor judge lands in the judge band. Solo accuracy does not predict what a layer adds: a cloud rule pack lowers the rule layer's solo miss rate by 20% and adds no new joint coverage. The difficulty share of judge coupling is not identifiable: 31.8% to 61.8% depending on the probe and the miss definition. One judge tier was served by an unrequested model version in 50 of 112 batches, concentrated on the external corpus. That event overturned a pre-declared analysis rule, and the scoring of review verdicts reversed five conclusions. We report both.

</details>


### [256] [MemCo: Memory-Centric Collaboration for Generalizing LLM Agents to Unseen Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07376)
*Xinting Liao,Siyan Liu,Rabab K. Ward,Holger R. Roth,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemCo：面向LLM智能体跨环境泛化的记忆中心协作框架，通过互补的局部/全局记忆空间与状态感知路由，提升交互决策任务成功率并减少冗余探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地在交互环境中做序贯决策。记忆可复用经验，但现有工作在孤立环境中设计记忆，收集足量轨迹代价高昂；跨任务/环境池化的共享记忆虽可缓解，但检索粒度存在两难：过细的记忆难以支撑当前环境接地，过粗的记忆无法指导下一步动作。

Method: 提出MemCo记忆中心协作框架：维护互补的局部与全局记忆空间，局部保留环境特有细节，全局从局部轨迹中归纳可迁移工作流；在线交互时依据智能体当前状态与决策阶段路由相关记忆，实现经验复用而不盲目迁移环境特定细节。

Result: 在交互式决策基准上，MemCo相较孤立记忆与共享记忆基线提升了任务成功率，并减少了冗余探索。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过分层记忆与状态感知检索实现跨环境经验迁移，验证了记忆粒度与结构设计对LLM智能体泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in interactive environments, where they need to make sequential decisions through observation, action, and feedback. Although memory can help agents reuse experience, existing work designs memory in isolation, where collecting enough trajectories to populate it is expensive. Existing shared-memory approaches mitigate isolated experience by pooling episodic memories across tasks and environments. However, retrieving shared memory is challenged by the granularity, where retrieved memories can be either too specific to preserve current grounding or too coarse to support the next action. In this work, we propose MemCo, a memory-centric collaboration framework for generalizing LLM agents to unseen interactive environments. It maintains complementary local and global memory spaces, preserving environment-specific details locally while promoting transferable workflows induced from local trajectories to global memory. During online interaction, MemCo routes relevant local and global memories in terms of the agent's current state and decision phase, enabling agents to reuse the experience of other agents without blindly transferring environment-specific details. Experiments on interactive decision-making benchmarks show that MemCo improves task success and reduces redundant exploration compared with isolate-memory and shared-memory baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/SYannL/nvdamas.

</details>


### [257] [Defense-in-Depth for LLMs: Evaluating Memory Gates Against Activation-Induced and Memory-Induced Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07403)
*Ritvij Sharma,Russell Dlugosz,Ryan Zhou,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出内-外部双层防御框架，分离内部激活引导与外部记忆处理，以缓解检索用户历史引发的记忆诱发谄媚。实验表明选择性路由门控过滤优于完全删除记忆，但逆向引导的额外增益未获统计显著支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使大模型能维持跨交互的个性化上下文，但检索到的用户历史会诱发"记忆诱发谄媚"，使模型偏向存储的用户信念而忽视客观证据。现有防御多作用于检索上下文，且很少与模型内部行为偏差联合评估，因此需要一种同时覆盖内部与外部机制的防御设计。

Method: 构建 $2\times2$ 深度防御框架，将内部激活引导与外部记忆处理分离。从100对配对提示中提取谄媚引导方向，在 MemSyco-Bench（全部1,550题作答，防御条件在固定250题子样本上评判）上评测四个开源模型、10个引导系数与五种记忆防御配置，并使用三个LLM评判器。五种配置中三种为新增（重写全部记忆、对每条记忆执行保留/重写/丢弃的路由门控、丢弃全部记忆），另两种为 MemSyco 基线。

Result: 选择性路由门控过滤相比完全移除记忆，能显著保留更多 MemSyco 平均准确率，且该分离在模型被引导向谄媚时依然存在。在 Llama 3.1 8B 配合路由门控下，温和逆向引导（$α=-1.5$）将评判器平均谄媚率从35.80%降至31.32%，同时平均准确率从43.99%变为43.31%；该下降在三个评判器下方向一致，但在149题上未达统计显著（配对 $p=0.08$ 至 $0.63$）。

Conclusion: 外部记忆过滤是设计中稳健有效的部分；数据未显示逆向引导能在此之上带来额外收益。因此防御的重心应放在外部记忆的选择性处理上，内部激活引导的增益尚需更强证据支持。

Abstract: Long-term memory allows Large Language Models (LLMs) to maintain personalized context across interactions, but retrieved user history can induce memory-induced sycophancy, causing models to favor stored user beliefs over objective evidence. Existing defenses primarily operate on retrieved context and are rarely evaluated jointly with internal behavioral bias. We introduce a $2 \times 2$ defense-in-depth framework separating internal activation steering from external memory handling. We extract sycophancy steering directions from 100 paired prompts and evaluate four open-weight models across 10 steering coefficients and five memory-defense configurations on MemSyco-Bench (answers for all 1,550 items; defense conditions judged on a fixed 250-item subsample), with three LLM judges. Three of the five configurations are new (rewriting every memory, a Router Gate that keeps, rewrites, or drops each memory, and dropping all memory); the other two are MemSyco's baselines. Selective Router Gate filtering preserves substantially more of MemSyco's average accuracy than complete memory removal, and this separation persists when the models are steered toward sycophancy. On Llama 3.1 8B with Router Gate, mild inverse steering ($α= -1.5$) lowers judge-averaged sycophancy from 35.80% to 31.32% while average accuracy moves from 43.99% to 43.31%; this reduction has the same direction under all three judges but is not statistically significant (paired $p = 0.08$ to $0.63$ on 149 items). External memory filtering is the part of the design that holds up; our data do not show that inverse steering adds to it.

</details>


### [258] [2d-fet-bench: from spatial reasoning to fet design on flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07423)
*Dunzhi Zhou,Chengyu Zhu,Gang Qiu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出2D-FET-Bench V2基准，用128个基于剥离二维薄片轮廓的版图任务测试语言模型智能体自动构建FET版图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维薄片FET版图通常需针对每个薄片手工绘制，逐片匹配其位置与轮廓，目前缺乏可执行基准来可靠检验语言模型智能体能否完成此类薄片特定版图构建。

Method: 构建由显微图像导出的薄片轮廓生成的128个版图任务（含带孔薄片与多薄片任务），每项提供文本器件规格与轮廓坐标；智能体生成类型化多边形与路径操作并渲染为GDSII；采用确定性验证器检查几何与结构要求，另设完整性检查确保轮廓不被篡改；以脚本参考版图验证全部任务可解；评测6个模型及GPT5.6-Luna的7种工作流/脚手架变体，每任务5次尝试。

Result: 脚本参考版图通过全部128个任务；最佳配置GPT5.6-Luna+ReAct-3通过62.3%的尝试，覆盖率80.5%（至少成功一次），一致性43.8%（五次全成功）；ReAct-3比单遍Plan-and-Execute的pass@1高27.0个百分点，但token消耗为其2.46倍；对覆盖任务中每个验证器通过版图抽样进行专家审计，五种ReAct-3配置接受率为56.4%–63.5%。

Conclusion: 该基准聚焦FET版图的几何与结构构建，可客观衡量语言模型智能体在薄片特定版图任务上的可靠性与一致性，并揭示验证器通过率与专家判定之间仍存在差距。

Abstract: Field-effect transistor (FET) layouts on exfoliated two-dimensional flakes are typically drawn by hand for each flake, placing contacts and gates to match its position and outline in optical micrographs. To our knowledge, no executable benchmark tests whether language-model agents can perform this flake-specific construction reliably. We introduce 2D-FET-Bench V2, a benchmark of 128 layout tasks built from microscopy-derived flake contours, including hole-containing flakes and multi-flake tasks. Each task supplies a textual device specification and contour coordinates. An agent generates typed polygon and path operations rendered to GDSII. A deterministic verifier checks geometric and structural requirements, and a separate integrity check verifies that the supplied contours remain unchanged. Scripted reference layouts pass all 128 tasks, showing that every task is solvable. We evaluate six models and seven workflow and scaffold variants of GPT5.6-Luna, with five attempts per task. The best-performing configuration in the six-model panel, GPT5.6-Luna with ReAct-3, passes 62.3% of attempts and solves 80.5% of tasks at least once (coverage) and 43.8% in all five attempts (consistency). ReAct-3 exceeds the one-pass Plan-and-Execute by 27.0 pass@1 points at 2.46 times the tokens. An expert audit of one sampled verifier-passing layout per covered task, across five ReAct-3 configurations, accepts 56.4% to 63.5% of them. The benchmark evaluates geometric and structural FET layout construction.

</details>


### [259] [Adaptive Gait Biofeedback With Participant-Held-Out Modeling and Participant-Specific Updating in Chronic Ankle Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07428)
*Jaeyoon,Kim,Veronika Lebisova,Jeniya Sultana,Jaeyoung Cho,Jaeho Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了用于慢性踝关节不稳自适应步态生物反馈的时序卷积分类器与人体响应：20人LOSO交叉验证显示AUROC 0.948、BAD敏感度0.941但GOOD特异度仅0.366；自适应组7人9次训练中候选模型BAD类F1提升；Post期自适应组额面踝角较对照低5.168度，但为非随机关联，不能确立因果获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性踝关节不稳患者可能受益于自适应步态生物反馈支持的重复练习，但对其评估需同时覆盖模型性能与人体响应两个方面，既往研究往往缺少此类双重评估。

Method: 采用协议定义、基于角度的GOOD/BAD步态周期标签，在20名受试者中使用留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）评估时序卷积分类器。自适应干预组7人三周完成9次训练，每次分析一次动作捕捉记录；对训练失败后更新的模型，与父模型在候选选择所用同次验证子集及首次后续自适应训练记录上进行离线比较。额面踝角在基线、Post及7天保留期与10名序贯入组对照进行比较。

Result: 20个留出折的折级AUROC均值为0.948，对超过角度阈值的BAD周期敏感度为0.941，对满足角度阈值的GOOD周期特异度仅0.366。候选模型的BAD类F1在同次子集上高0.187，在首次后续记录上高0.118。Post期自适应组基线校正后额面踝角较对照低5.168度（95%CI 1.766–8.569），保留期对比结果不确定。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了重复生物反馈使用中的群体模型判别能力与离线个体化更新效果，并发现非随机化的Post期额面踝角关联；但不能确立独立的临床步态分类能力，也不能证明模型更新带来因果获益。

Abstract: Adaptive gait biofeedback may support repeated practice in chronic ankle instability, but its evaluation must address model performance and human response. We evaluated a temporal convolutional classifier on protocol-defined, angle-derived GOOD/BAD gait-cycle labels using participant-held-out leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation in 20 participants. Seven participants in the adaptive-intervention group completed nine sessions over three weeks, with one motion-capture recording analyzed per session. Models updated after failed sessions were compared offline with their parent models on the same-session validation subset used for candidate selection and the first subsequent adaptive-session recording. Frontal-plane ankle angle was compared between the adaptive group and 10 sequentially enrolled controls at Baseline, Post, and 7-day Retention. Across 20 held-out folds, mean fold-level area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) was 0.948, sensitivity for angle-threshold-exceeding BAD cycles was 0.941, and specificity for angle-threshold-meeting GOOD cycles was 0.366. Mean BAD-class F1 was higher in candidate models by 0.187 on the same-session subset and 0.118 on the first subsequent recording. At Post, the adaptive group had a baseline-adjusted frontal-plane ankle angle 5.168 degrees lower than controls (95% confidence interval, 1.766-8.569 degrees lower); the Retention contrast was uncertain. These findings characterize population-model discrimination and offline participant-specific updating during repeated biofeedback use, alongside a nonrandomized Post frontal-plane ankle angle association. They do not establish independent clinical gait classification or a causal benefit of updating.

</details>


### [260] [When Does AI Supervision Help? A Role-Aware Study of Network Fraud Decision Management with Blockchain Auditability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07434)
*Saviz Changizi,Nasibeh Mohammadzadeh,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过具备区块链审计能力的"决策者-监督者"（DS）框架，评估四种由集中式机器学习、FedAvg联邦元模型及Base/QLoRA大模型变体组成的方向性配置，探讨第二个AI组件何时真正提升网络欺诈决策而非增加运营负担。结果表明，AI监督的价值取决于角色分配、校准、升级策略、流量构成与生命周期控制，而非仅取决于第二个模型的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于回答一个实践性问题：在网络欺诈决策中，引入第二个AI组件何时能改善主决策，而不是徒增运营负担。为此需要一个可审计的评估框架，系统考察不同角色分配与模型组合下监督机制的实际收益。

Method: 方法上提出角色感知的Decider-Supervisor（DS）框架，并引入区块链审计能力，构建四种方向性配置，组合集中式机器学习、FedAvg训练的联邦元模型以及Base或QLoRA大语言模型变体。评估维度包括：非硬欺诈（non-hard）性能、干预负担、条件校准、流量构成与审核容量敏感性、可靠性测试，以及区块链生命周期控制。其中确定性硬门控先处理大部分欺诈请求，剩余非硬样本成为AI决策的主要场景。

Result: 结果显示：确定性硬门控解决了89.994%的欺诈请求；条件验证校准在部署回放中未产生可稳定迁移的监督优势；DS-3 QLoRA虽为干扰最小的监督配置，但在F1和总错误数上仍逊于其主FedAvg阶段；在36种重加权流量混合中，仅DS-4 Base在两种极端高欺诈场景下通过监督降低了总错误。区块链测试支持摘要校验、篡改检测、授权、单次评审决议及终结后完整性，同时暴露出终结前单写限制。

Conclusion: 结论指出，AI监督的价值并非源自第二个模型本身的存在，而取决于角色分配、校准、升级策略、流量构成以及生命周期控制等多种因素的协同。研究为网络欺诈场景中是否以及如何引入监督型AI提供了条件性判断依据。

Abstract: When does a second artificial intelligence (AI) component improve a primary network-fraud decision rather than add operational burden? We study this question through a role-aware Decider-Supervisor (DS) framework with blockchain auditability, evaluating four directional configurations that combine centralised machine learning, a Federated Averaging (FedAvg)-trained federated meta-model, and Base or Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA) large language model variants. The analysis compares primary-only and supervised decisions using non-hard fraud performance, intervention burden, conditional calibration, traffic-mix and Review-capacity sensitivity, dependability tests, and blockchain lifecycle controls. The deterministic hard gate resolves 89.994% of fraudulent requests, leaving the non-hard population as the main AI decision setting. Conditional validation calibration does not produce a consistently transferable supervisory advantage on deployment replay. DS-3 QLoRA is the least disruptive supervised configuration, but it still underperforms its primary FedAvg stage in F1 and total errors. Across 36 reweighted traffic mixtures, supervision reduces total errors only for DS-4 Base in two extreme high-fraud scenarios. Blockchain tests support digest verification, tamper detection, authorisation, single-use review resolution, and post-finalisation integrity, while exposing a pre-finalisation single-write limitation. The results show that the value of AI supervision depends on role assignment, calibration, escalation policy, traffic composition, and lifecycle controls rather than on the presence of a second model alone.

</details>


### [261] [Auditable Claims about AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07459)
*Yue Zhao,Jiate Li,Li Li,Yi Nian,Jinbo Liu,Xiaolin Zhou,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"可审计声明"（auditable claim）概念，要求AI代理的声明在裁决前固定其策略、范围、可裁决记录及记录责任人，并证明代理场景下需额外满足独立记录覆盖、授权绑定动作参数、完整性超越数据完整性三个条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织常对AI代理作出如"每封外部邮件均有人员审批""每个动作均被记录"等声明，但《欧盟人工智能法案》第12条仅要求高风险系统支持自动记录事件，并未规定哪些记录足以支持某一声明，导致声明难以被验证。因此需建立一种使声明可被审计的前置条件框架。

Method: 借鉴保证业务（assurance engagement）的前置条件，定义"可审计声明"：其构成要素与裁决规则须在任何结论作出前确定，且记录可获取。将Auditable Agents框架中的Policy Checkability维度从单一动作扩展到声明层面；针对代理特性增设三个条件——独立记录覆盖、授权绑定每个动作的参数、超越完整性的完备性。在显式模型下证明：当假设成立时，缺少任一条件则支持不可行。并将方法应用于一张声明核查表，覆盖六类常见声明，以NIST、IETF、OWASP现行草案为锚定依据。

Result: 在显式模型下证明：若缺少独立记录覆盖、参数级授权绑定或超越完整性的完备性任一条件，则无法对声明提供支持。声明核查表将方法应用于六类常见声明，并以一个具体案例贯穿五种证据状态进行演示。

Conclusion: 研究给出面向操作方、采购方、审计方与标准制定方的实践框与操作步骤，推动AI代理声明在裁决前明确策略、范围、记录与责任人，从而实现可审计性。

Abstract: Organizations make claims about their AI agents: a person approves every external email, every action is logged, an evaluation shows the agent is safe to deploy. Article 12 of the EU AI Act requires high-risk systems to allow the automatic recording of events but does not say which records settle a given claim. The position is one sentence: to be checked, a claim about an agent must first name its policy, its scope, the records that would settle it, and who writes them. Adapting the preconditions of an assurance engagement, we call a claim auditable when these elements and a decision rule are fixed before any verdict and the records are obtainable. This extends the Policy Checkability dimension of our Auditable Agents framework from single actions to claims. Agents add three conditions: coverage by an independent record, authorization bound to each action's arguments, and completeness beyond integrity. Under an explicit model, we prove that support is impossible without each wherever its hypotheses hold. A claim-check table applies the method to six common claims, anchored in current NIST, IETF, and OWASP drafts. A worked case follows one claim through five evidence states. We close with a practice box and steps for operators, buyers, auditors, and standard setters.

</details>


### [262] [COMPASS: Finding Where Reasoning Lives in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07469)
*Pratyay Dutta,Kowshik Thopalli,Vivek Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 COMPASS，一种推理时激活引导方法：利用模型自身直接作答的正确性作为信号，构造引导推理的潜在方向，并通过 logit 空间归因分数定位少数可干预的注意力头，从而实现少 token、高精度的数学推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 eliciting reasoning 方法依赖对推理的预定义刻画（如 CoT prompt design、contrastive CoT directions、SAE 提取的推理特征），先验假设较强、实现复杂。作者希望证明：在答案可验证的数学推理任务中，一个更简单的信号——模型自身直接作答是否正确——就足以诱导推理方向。

Method: 1) 用模型直接答题的正确性作为监督信号，构造潜在"正确性方向"；2) 发现该方向在多数注意力头的激活中可解码，但仅少数头可被有效干预；3) 提出 COMPASS：用 logit 空间归因分数识别可干预头，沿正确性方向对其激活进行推理时引导，仅需逐头激活统计量。

Result: 在三个模型族与多个数学基准上，COMPASS 优于所比较的激活引导基线；GSM8K 平均提升 16 个百分点；以少 20–70% 的生成 token 逼近 CoT 精度；干预可迁移至未见基准而无需重新拟合；消融实验表明正确性方向与承载它的少量头均为必要，且效果集中在极少数头上。

Conclusion: 简单的"自身作答正确性"信号即可替代复杂的推理刻画来引导 LLM 推理；激活引导的有效性高度依赖于少数特定注意力头，COMPASS 为低成本、可迁移的推理增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Explicitly eliciting reasoning substantially improves LLM performance. Existing approaches require a predefined characterization of reasoning, whether through CoT prompt design, contrastive CoT directions, or via SAE derived reasoning features. For mathematical reasoning with verifiable answers, we show that a much simpler signal suffices, which is the correctness of the model's own direct answer attempts. This signal yields a latent direction that elicits reasoning. This direction is decodable within the activations of most attention heads, but only a small subset of them can be effectively intervened. We introduce COMPASS, an inference-time steering method that identifies these heads using a logit-space attribution score and steers their activations along the correctness direction, requiring only per-head activation statistics. Across three model families and multiple math benchmarks, COMPASS outperforms the activation-steering baselines we compare against, improves GSM8K accuracy by 16 percentage points on average, and approaches CoT accuracy with 20-70\% fewer generated tokens. Interventions transfer without re-fitting to unseen benchmarks, and ablations show that both the correctness direction and the small set of heads carrying it are necessary, with the effect concentrated in remarkably few heads.

</details>


### [263] [PsyCIDRA: A Dual-Agent Framework for Psychiatric Interviewing and Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07473)
*Milad Mohammadi,Fatemeh Akrami Shamsabadi,Zahra Mohseni,Amirhossein Safdarian,Malekfarhad Malek,Hadi Moradi,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出PsyCIDRA双智能体框架，将自由形式精神科访谈与诊断推理相衔接：访谈智能体借助工具维护工作笔记、加载专家技能并检索ICD-11参考；诊断推理智能体基于完整访谈记录给出假设及支持、冲突与缺失证据，在证据不足时保留最终假设。在模拟与人类盲法研究中均优于直接提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床推理方面展现出潜力，但精神科访谈需要引导一场不断演化的对话，其进行此类交互式评估的能力尚缺乏研究。因此需要考察LLM智能体在自由形式对话中辅助精神科评估的能力与局限。

Method: 提出PsyCIDRA双智能体框架。访谈智能体使用工具维护工作笔记、加载专家撰写的技能、检索ICD-11参考以引导问诊；诊断推理智能体接收完成的访谈记录，报告诊断假设及支持性、冲突性和缺失证据，并在无充分支持时不给最终假设。使用PsyCPG生成的患者画像，先在仿真中评估，再进行人类盲法研究（101名参与者，分为不同组别）。

Result: 在53个评估案例的四个模型上，诊断一致性高于直接提示；在81个留出模拟案例上，rank-1准确率为60.5%，对比直接提示的51.9%。在101名人类参与者的盲法研究中，PsyCIDRA在是否提出诊断假设上与心理学家的意见一致率为79.6%，直接提示为65.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持LLM智能体通过自由形式对话辅助精神科评估的潜力。通过同时考察诊断推理、访谈质量和安全性，本研究有助于理解精神科访谈智能体的能力与局限。

Abstract: Large language models show promise in clinical reasoning, but psychiatric interviewing requires guiding an evolving conversation. Their ability to carry out this interactive assessment remains less studied. We present PsyCIDRA, a dual-agent framework linking free-form psychiatric interviewing with diagnostic reasoning for expert review. Its interviewer agent uses tools to maintain working notes, load expert-written skills, and retrieve ICD-11 references to guide inquiry. Its diagnostic reasoning agent then receives the completed interview transcript and reports hypotheses alongside supporting, conflicting, and missing evidence, withholding a final hypothesis when none is sufficiently supported. Using patient profiles generated with PsyCPG, we first evaluate PsyCIDRA in simulation. Across four models on 53 evaluation cases, it achieves higher diagnostic agreement than direct prompting. On 81 held-out simulated cases, rank-1 accuracy is 60.5% versus 51.9%. In a blinded study of 101 human participants in separate arms, PsyCIDRA agrees with psychologists on whether to propose a diagnostic hypothesis in 79.6% of cases, compared with 65.4% for direct prompting. Together, these findings support the potential of LLM agents to assist psychiatric assessment through free-form dialogue. By examining diagnostic reasoning, interview quality, and safety together, this study contributes to understanding the capabilities and limitations of psychiatric interview agents.

</details>


### [264] [In With the Old: Enhancing 'Classical' Document Automation with Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07480)
*Marc Lauritsen,Hannes Westermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨基于专家系统等符号方法的文档自动化法律服务如何与当前生成式AI相互增强，并对用大语言模型识别与修正非专业用户文书问题进行了初步实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件法律辅助系统已采用多种知识表示与推理形式，但符号方法与生成式AI之间的关系尚待厘清，需明确二者结合的价值与障碍。

Method: 结合专家系统风格及符号方法的文档自动化服务与生成式AI方法进行概念性分析，并开展使用大语言模型识别和修正非专业人士文本问题的初步实验。

Result: 初探性实验显示大语言模型可用于识别和修正非专业用户所撰文本中的问题，同时揭示了符号方法辅助生成式AI及被其增强的潜在收益与挑战。

Conclusion: 符号式文档自动化与生成式AI可在法律辅助场景中互补增强，但需进一步研究其可行性与局限。

Abstract: Software-based legal assistance systems have leveraged many different forms of knowledge representation and reasoning. This article explores how document automation services rooted in expert system style and other symbolic approaches can usefully enhance and be enhanced by current generative AI approaches. We discuss the possible benefits and challenges, and report on preliminary experiments in using large language models to identify and fix issues in texts written by laypeople.

</details>


### [265] [Does Muon Need Fine-Grained Spectral Shaping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07497)
*Meher Chaitanya,Tianyi Zhou,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BulkBoost两带谱重加权框架，发现Muon更新中约94–97%的奇异模式位于噪声边缘之下却整体与参考梯度正对齐，仅需一个bulk-to-spike增益即可媲美Freon的细粒度谱剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon的极坐标更新Q=UV^T对每个奇异方向赋予相同权重（flat profile），而近期优化器（如Freon、Spectra）改用细粒度谱映射为每个方向设置独立增益。作者质疑：这种谱细节究竟有多少是必要的？谱诊断显示约94–97%的奇异模式位于估计噪声边缘之下，却整体上正向对齐参考梯度，暗示有效偏离可能具有低维结构。

Method: 提出BulkBoost两带谱重加权框架，含固定秩与噪声校准两种变体。噪声校准变体利用split-minibatch梯度差为Muon的Nesterov输入校准Marchenko–Pastur参考边缘，将边缘以下的bulk与以上的spike分离；两种变体通过一个共享增益提升bulk相对权重，同时保持各矩阵未重加权方向的Frobenius范数。理论给出在固定划分下，将权重移向bulk可降低损失的一阶条件，并量化两带划分能捕获的最大一阶改进速率的比例。

Result: 在Pythia-14M至410M、六个语料库共30个持续预训练设置中，两带重加权与Freon的细粒度幂律剖面竞争，并优于Spectra。相对Muon的flat profile，Freon平均降低最终损失0.022%（占适配前损失比例），而两带变体降低0.073%–0.147%。

Conclusion: 有效偏离flat profile的方向出人意料地低维：单个bulk-to-spike增益所能带来的收益不低于细粒度谱剖面，说明Muon的谱细节中大部分对优化性能并非必需。

Abstract: Muon combines current and past gradients into matrix momentum. For $M=UΣV^\top$, the idealized polar update $Q=UV^\top$ gives every singular direction the same weight. We refer to this as the flat profile. Several recent optimizers replace this flat profile with fine-grained spectral maps that give each direction its own gain. We ask how much of this spectral detail a Muon update needs. Our spectral diagnostics show that approximately $94$--$97\%$ of measured singular modes lie below an estimated noise edge, yet collectively align positively with a reference gradient.
  We introduce BulkBoost, a two-band spectral reweighting framework with fixed-rank and noise-calibrated variants. The latter uses split-minibatch gradient differences to calibrate a Marchenko--Pastur reference edge for Muon's Nesterov input, separating the bulk below the edge from the spikes above it. Both variants increase the bulk's relative weight through one shared gain while preserving the Frobenius norm of each matrix's unreweighted direction. For a fixed partition, our theory gives the first-order condition under which moving weight toward the bulk lowers the loss. It also quantifies the fraction of the maximal first-order improvement rate, over all per-mode reallocations, that two bands can capture. Across 30 continued-pretraining settings spanning Pythia-14M to 410M and six corpora, two-band reweighting is competitive with the fine-grained power-law profile of Freon and outperforms Spectra. Measured against Muon's flat profile, Freon reduces final loss by $0.022\%$ of the pre-adaptation loss on average, whereas the two-band variants achieve reductions of $0.073$--$0.147\%$. These observations suggest that useful departures from the flat profile are surprisingly low-dimensional: a single bulk-to-spike gain captures at least as much benefit as the fine-grained spectral profiles.

</details>


### [266] [MARS: Multi-resolution Adaptive Routing for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07505)
*Ming Yin,Sixun Dong,Yudong Liu,Wen-Yun Yang,Yunjiang Jiang,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MARS，一种多分辨率用户记忆机制，通过多时间尺度循环状态轨道与稀疏路由读取器，解决长历史推荐中缓存记忆对各时间尺度保留不均的"时序混叠"问题，在保持固定大小候选打分的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长历史推荐系统通常将用户历史压缩为与候选项无关的紧凑缓存记忆以复用打分，但真实用户历史存在多尺度语义结构（短期意图、中期兴趣、长期偏好共存）。单体缓存记忆对这些尺度的保留不均衡，线性探针显示近期和中期内容恢复效果远差于长期内容，作者将这一失效模式称为"时序混叠"。

Method: MARS将完整历史写入锚定于不同半衰期的循环状态轨道，实现多分辨率记忆；并设计稀疏路由读取器，为每个种子选择相关的时间分辨率，物化出紧凑的种子记忆，从而保持固定大小候选打分。

Result: MARS在三个公开数据集上优于强基线，且增益随历史长度增加而扩大。组件匹配消融与配对检验显示，时间多样性与选择性路由各自带来超出硬窗口记忆或增加容量所能提供的贡献。在用户内部行为漂移后，MARS相对接口匹配基线的优势进一步扩大，每用户1000个候选时服务延迟约为该基线热缓存延迟的1.02倍。

Conclusion: 多分辨率记忆与稀疏路由是提升长历史推荐的有效途径，能在不牺牲服务效率的前提下更好地保留用户历史的多尺度语义结构，并在行为漂移场景下表现出更强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-history recommenders often compress each user's history into a compact, candidate-independent memory that is cached and reused to score large candidate pools. We show that real user histories exhibit multi-scale semantic structure, with short-lived intent, medium-term interests, and long-term preferences coexisting in one sequence, and that monolithic cached memories preserve these scales unevenly: linear probes recover recent and mid-range content far worse than long-range content. We call this failure mode \textit{temporal aliasing}. We propose \textbf{MARS}, a multi-resolution user memory that writes the full history into recurrent state tracks anchored to different half-lives, and a sparse routing reader that materializes compact seed memories by selecting the relevant temporal resolutions for each seed, preserving fixed-size candidate scoring. MARS outperforms strong baselines on three public datasets, with gains that grow with history length. Component-matched ablations with paired tests show that temporal diversity and selective routing each contribute beyond what hard-window memories or added capacity provide. The advantage of MARS over its interface-matched baseline also widens after within-user behavioral shifts, at about $1.02\times$ that baseline's warm-cache serving latency for $1{,}000$ candidates per user.

</details>


### [267] [On Open-Ended Information Seeking for Information Elicitation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07509)
*Victor De Lima,Grace Hui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究不同大语言模型在信息引导任务中对"信息价值"判断的差异，以及这些差异如何塑造序贯信息搜寻的广度-深度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息引导是开放式信息搜寻问题，交互可向多个潜在有价值的方向展开，引导者需在新信息涌现时持续判断应追求哪些信息。在智能体引导中，这些决策可能被委托给基础模型，但模型选择如何影响信息搜寻行为仍缺乏研究。

Method: 首先在11个跨多个模型家族和参数规模的LLM上，使用共享的信息集和引导目标考察信息价值判断；随后构建受控引导仿真，让不同模型面对相同信息空间并使用相同选择规则，从而将判断与问题生成及被访者行为隔离；在此基础上刻画由模型特定信息搜寻偏好产生的广度-深度行为，并考察交互历史如何改变对候选信息的评估与后续选择；最后通过敏感性分析和消融实验检验机会数量、响应标签、交互历史存在与否以及冗余相关性等边界条件。

Result: 模型间的信息价值判断存在差异，并在受控仿真中导致不同的广度-深度搜寻模式；交互历史会改变对候选信息的评估和选择；上述发现在多组敏感性分析与消融中表现稳健并刻画了其边界。

Conclusion: LLM的选择会系统性塑造智能体信息引导的行为特征，信息价值判断是可测量的模型特定属性；项目代码、数据与轨迹文件已开源。

Abstract: Information elicitation is an open-ended information-seeking problem in which an interaction can unfold in many potentially valuable directions, requiring an elicitor to continually determine which information to pursue as new information emerges. In agentic elicitation, these decisions may be delegated to a foundation model, yet how model choice shapes the resulting information-seeking behavior remains understudied. We study how judgments about information value vary across LLMs and how these differences shape sequential information seeking. We first examine these judgments across 11 LLMs spanning multiple model families and parameter scales, using a shared set of information and elicitation objectives. We then develop a controlled elicitation simulation in which different models encounter the same information space and use the same selection rule, isolating these judgments from question generation and respondent behavior. Using this setting, we characterize the breadth-depth behavior that emerges from model-specific information-seeking preferences over the course of elicitation. We further examine how interaction history changes the evaluation and subsequent selection of prospective information. We test the robustness and boundaries of these findings through sensitivity analyses and ablations over the opportunities available to the elicitor, the response labels used to operationalize information-seeking preferences, the presence of interaction history, and whether redundancy is explicitly relevant to the assessment. The project code, data, and trajectory files are available at https://github.com/infosenselab/open-elicitation.

</details>


### [268] [From Local Evidence to Safety Verdicts: Causal Tracing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07514)
*Faezeh Dehghan Tarzjani,Mevan Wijewardena,Alexander Romanus,Sampad Mohanty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SSU-Bench数据集，通过对配对安全/不安全图像-文本组合进行内部状态迁移干预，发现视觉语言模型的联合安全判断存在跨层迁移：变更输入位置的干预在较早解码层有效，最终输入token的干预在较晚层有效，并区分了"可读出的模型决策"与"正确的安全判断"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型需要结合图像与提示才能识别单独任一方无法揭示的安全风险，但这种联合安全判断在模型内部何处形成、何时变得可干预尚不清楚。论文旨在定位这一联合安全判断在模型内部的可及位置，并厘清模型决策与真实安全判断之间的差别。

Method: 构建SSU-Bench数据集，使用单项提示编辑或带标注目标区域的图像编辑，生成配对的安全与不安全图像-文本组合。在三个视觉语言模型上，对配对输入进行内部状态迁移干预，测量安全判决的变化；并在不同解码层、不同输入位置施加干预，同时用其他样本估计的方向向量进行对照，另用最终token状态的线性读出预测模型判决。

Result: 在多个模型和两类反事实下，针对变更输入位置的干预在较早解码层有效，而针对最终输入token的干预在较晚层才有效；由其他样本估计的方向产生类似的晚层效应；最终token状态的线性读出能够预测模型自身判决（包括错误判决）；跨模型比较显示反事实变化模式具有相似性。

Conclusion: 研究识别出联合安全判决中干预有效性位置的反复出现的层级转变，并将可读出的模型决策与正确的安全判断区分开来，为理解视觉语言模型安全判断的内部机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: A vision-language model may need to combine an image with a prompt to recognize a safety risk that neither reveals alone. Where does this joint safety judgment become accessible inside the model? We introduce SSU-Bench, a dataset of matched safe and unsafe image-text combinations constructed using single-item prompt edits or image edits with annotated intended regions. Using three vision-language models, we transfer internal states between paired inputs and measure the resulting change in the safety verdict. Across models and both types of counterfactual, interventions at the changed input positions are effective in earlier decoder layers, while interventions at the final input token become effective later. Directions estimated from other examples produce similar late-layer effects. A linear readout of the final-token state also predicts the model's own verdict, including incorrect judgments, and cross-model comparisons reveal similarities in the patterns of counterfactual change. These findings identify a recurring transition in where interventions can influence a joint safety verdict and distinguish a readable model decision from a correct safety judgment.

</details>


### [269] [Grounding What Shapes the Plan: Rethinking Groundedness for Physical Intelligence in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07521)
*Minkyoung Cho,Zewei Zhou,Wenhao Ding,Shuhan Tan,Boyi Li,Yuxiao Chen,Yan Wang,Zheng Lian,Min-Hung Chen,Chaowei Xiao,Zhuoqing Mao,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yulong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GroundAct，将"实体的连续状态"作为推理落地（grounding）的单元，通过轻量引用 token 将符号推理与实体连续状态关联，并仅用被引用实体的交互来修正规划，形成"从推理依据到规划动作"的显式路径（grounded planning），在开环与闭环评测中均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模型虽将推理建立在因果关系、空间结构、感知证据与预测未来之上，使推理更贴合驾驶场景，但未回答一个根本问题：当模型最终输出动作时，"落地性"应意味着什么。推理正确并不必然带来理想的驾驶结果，因此需要一种使推理依据直接约束规划动作的机制。

Method: GroundAct 以物理实体及其交互为出发点：1) 以实体作为 grounding 的基本单元；2) 使用轻量引用 token（reference token）使每个被选中实体的连续状态在符号推理过程中保持可寻址；3) 仅让被引用实体与不断演化的候选规划之间的交互来修正规划。由此建立从"推理落地对象"到"规划行为"的显式路径，即 grounded planning。

Result: 在开环与闭环两种设置下评估 GroundAct。开环规划在正常、分布外（OOD）以及安全关键场景中均表现强劲；闭环结果进一步将该证据扩展到仿真驾驶中。

Conclusion: 将 grounding 的单元落在实体及其交互上、并以引用 token 关联连续状态与符号推理，能够使驾驶模型的推理与规划动作形成显式联系，从而在多样场景与仿真闭环驾驶中取得良好效果。

Abstract: Driving models increasingly ground reasoning in causal relations, spatial structure, perceptual evidence, and predicted futures. These advances make reasoning more faithful to the driving scene, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: what should groundedness mean when the model ultimately outputs an action? Correctly grounded reasoning does not, by itself, ensure desirable driving outcomes. We introduce GroundAct, which starts from a simple premise: driving unfolds through physical entities and their interactions. Entities therefore become the unit of grounding; a lightweight reference token keeps each selected entity's continuous state addressable through symbolic reasoning; and only the referenced entities' interactions with the evolving proposal correct the plan. The result is an explicit path from what reasoning grounds to what the plan does, which we call grounded planning. To assess its practical value, we evaluate GroundAct in both open- and closed-loop settings. GroundAct shows strong open-loop planning across normal, out-of-distribution, and safety-critical scenarios, with closed-loop results extending this evidence to driving in simulation.

</details>


### [270] [A Systematic Investigation of Bias in Large Language Models for Advertising Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07544)
*Weiwei Wang,Yinchuan Xu,Jialu Gao,Youkow Homma,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM广告相关性判断中的公平性问题，通过反事实框架检验广告商身份、语言、人口统计措辞的影响，并探索缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于判断广告与查询的匹配度，但其判断的公平性受到的关注有限，存在公平性风险未被充分认识的问题。

Method: 采用反事实框架，研究广告商身份、流行度、输入语言及人口统计措辞的影响。以GPT-4o作为分类相关性评判者，以及专门训练用于相关性预测的Qwen-7B模型为对象。广告商与语言实验使用真实广告日志中的查询-广告对；人口统计关联研究使用受控合成查询，涵盖就业、住房和信贷领域。同时研究推理和训练阶段的缓解方法。

Result: 对两个模型而言，改变广告商身份或输入语言都会改变相关性评估；部分人口统计比较显示出与常见刻板印象一致的模式，尤其是涉及性别与职业的对比。缓解方法的效果取决于广告商信息是否与查询相关以及训练数据中广告商标签的分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果可帮助广告从业者识别公平性风险，并为LLM相关性系统开发合适的缓解方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to judge how well an advertisement matches a query, but the fairness of these judgments has received limited attention. We conduct a systematic study of fairness in relevance judgments made by LLMs for queries and advertisements.
